# ATLAS reading packet

Collected 2026-11-24T04:07:54+00:00. This file is context for the editor. It is not the edition.
Each item is a headline already fetched from a feed or a newsroom page. A text-reader item was taken from a page that blocks a normal download.
High confidence is still three independent sources, or five when the national-language and official mix is missing. Medium is two. Low is one, and the summary must say so.

#### Agência Brasil (national_press, pt, feed)

- Mon, 23 Nov 2026 21:03:00 -0300 — Petrobras aprova pagamento de R$ 15,6 bilhões a acionistas
  O Conselho de Administração da Petrobras, em reunião realizada nesta segunda-feira (23), aprovou o pagamento de remuneração extraordinária aos acionistas no valor de R$ 15, 6 bilhões, equivalente a R$ 1,21035949 por ação ordinária e preferencial em circulação. O montante será pago em parcela única e integralmente sob a forma de dividendos intercalares (durante o próprio exercício em curso, antes do fechamento do balanço anual da empresa). Para os detentores de ações de emissão da Petrobras negociadas na B3, a data-base será 7 de dezembro de 2026. As ações da Petrobras serão negociadas ex-direitos a partir do dia seguinte, 8 de dezembro de 2026. Notícias relacionadas: Petrobras tem lucro de R$ 39,8 bilhões no 3º trimestre, alta de 21,5%. O valor será pago em parcela única no dia 23 de dezem
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/economia/noticia/2026-11/petrobras-aprova-pagamento-de-r-156-bilhoes-acionistas

  VA271: Launch kit for the 10th Ariane 6 launch VA271: Launch kit for the 10th Ariane 6 launch News 23.11.2026 The 10th Ariane 6 launch will take place on 24 November. On the VA271 mission, the European heavy-lift launch vehicle will place 36 Amazon Leo satellites into low Earth orbit (LEO). &nbsp; This is the fifth Ariane 6 launch of 2026 and the second to use P160C-powered boosters. This upgrade increases the payload capacity of the Ariane 64 (the Ariane 6 version with four booster rockets) to around 22 tonnes into low Earth orbit. - undated — Monitoring the Coasts With Near-Daily Satellite Data This study is a decade-scale demonstration of the value of high-frequency imagery in monitoring and protecting dynamic shorelines.
  Monitoring the Coasts With Near-Daily Satellite Data HOME Publications Monitoring the Coasts With Near-Daily Satellite Data Austin Stone November 23, 2026 • 3 min read PlanetScope image of the Everglades coastline in Florida, captured May 15, 2025. © 2026, Planet Labs PBC. All Rights reserved. Publications Publications Satellite imagery is a critical resource in monitoring the health of coastal environments that are increasingly vulnerable to the impacts of climate and land use change. However, these ecosystems are so dynamic that many short-lived events can be missed between image captures by satellites that provide resolutions coarser than 10 meters with multiday revisit times, such as Landsat and Sentinel-2. While high-resolution aerial flights or drone surveys offer sub-meter-scale detail, they are costly, localized, and impossible to deploy systematically across entire national coas
  https://www.planet.com/pulse/monitoring-the-coasts-with-near-daily-satellite-data

#### Thales Alenia Space news (company, en, newsroom)

- undated — Hera paving the way for a new chapter in planetary defence
  Hera paving the way for a new chapter in planetary defence | Thales Alenia Space Skip to main content Next Thales Alenia Space ... What would happen if an asteroid the size of the Great Pyramid of Giza was on a collision course with Earth? Its impact could destroy entire cities, or generate a devastating tsunami if it struck the ocean. Would humanity be prepared to respond to such a threat? That is precisely what the European Space Agency's Hera mission aims to help determine. On September 26 th 2022, NASA’s Double Asteroid Redirection Test (DART) mission carried out humanity’s first-ever asteroid deflection experiment by deliberately crashing its 580 kg spacecraft into Dimorphos, a moonlet measuring 151 meters in diameter, at a speed of 6.1 km/s. The impact generated a trail of debris stretching more than 10,000 km into space and altered Dimorphos’ orbit around its parent body, the 780-
  https://www.thalesaleniaspace.com/en/news/hera-paving-way-new-chapter-planetary-defence
- undated — Thales Alenia Space Takes a New Step Towards Europe’s Future Quantum Satellite Internet with the TeQuantS Project
  Thales Alenia Space Takes a New Step Towards Europe’s Future Quantum Satellite Internet with the TeQuantS Project | Thales Alenia Space Skip to main content Next Preparing the ExoMar... Thales Alenia Space, the joint venture between Thales (67%) and Leonardo (33%), has signed two contract amendments with the European Space Agency (ESA), as prime contractor for the TeQuantS [1] project, aimed at developing quantum communication technologies between Space and Earth. The project is part of the Industrial Competitiveness component of ESA’s ARTES program [2] , supported by the French Space Agency (CNES). Launched in 2023, the TeQuantS project aims to develop the space-based quantum technologies required to build the future long-distance quantum Internet network. The initial phases of the project have enabled the development of a compact ground station prototype and a first engineering model o
  https://www.thalesaleniaspace.com/en/news/thales-alenia-space-takes-new-step-towards-europes-future-quantum-satellite-internet-tequants

  ### [Nasa is looking for new astronauts to send to the moon, and I know who’d be perfect for the job: middle-aged women like me | Helen Pilcher](https://www.theguardian.com/commentisfree/2026/nov/23/nasa-astronauts-moon-science-crew-member-women) We’ve been training for this mission for years – solving problems, defusing tensions, keeping everyone fed – and we’d absolutely smash it Nasa is hiring and I’m over the moon. The US space agency is recruiting its [next cohort of astronauts](https://www.theguardian.com/science/2026/nov/08/nasa-astronauts-moon-applications-open) to work on the lunar surface and “help write the next chapter of humanity’s greatest adventure”. Applications close on 3 December. This isn’t the only job I’ve been swooning over lately. - Mon, 23 Nov 2026 18:59:42 +0000 — Ultra-Thin "Rainbow" Sails Could Power Spacecraft and Shade the Earth
  For years, whimsically minded space enthusiasts have been dreaming of sailing across the vastness of the void on beams of sunlight. Photons, the massless particles that make up those beams of sunlight, still carry momentum, and can therefore impart a gentle push when they bounce off a surface. This force is the concept behind “solar sailing”, which has been having a bit of a moment in the last decade as several proof-of-concept flights have shown it is indeed actually feasible. Now a new paper, available in pre-print on arXiv from researchers at the Rochester Institute of Technology (RIT), has unveiled a clever technique that will allow these giant sails to steer sideways using a technology more commonly associated with flat-screen displays.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/ultra-thin-rainbow-sails-could-power-spacecraft-and-shade-the-earth

  LISA, the ESA's Laser Interferometry Space Antenna, is being built to detect gravitational waves emitted when massive objects spiral together and merge. New research shows that exoplanets can exert a small but perceptible tug on those objects, leaving a signature on the gravitational waves. In some cases, LISA should be able to detect their signatures.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/lisas-gravitational-wave-detections-could-reveal-exoplanets

  As we gear up to eventually land a human on Mars, more and more ideas are coming out about how to prepare for that.   La Nación / Restaurante paraguayo ingresa al ranking extendido de Latin America 50 Best y proyecta la gastronomía nacional La gastronomía paraguaya gana terreno en el mercado internacional y suma nuevas oportunidades para proyectar al país como destino turístico y de negocios. El restaurante asunceno Óga ingresó al puesto 100 de la lista ampliada de The 50 Best Restaurants Latin America 2026 , un reconocimiento que devuelve a Paraguay a esta clasificación después de diez años y amplía la visibilidad de su oferta gastronómica en la región. La organización dio a conocer la denominada Extended List, que comprende a los establecimientos ubicados entre los puestos 51 y 100 . El ranking principal será presentado el próximo 1 de diciembre en Guadalajara, México, durante una ceremonia que reunirá a referentes de la industria gastronómica latinoamericana. - Mon, 23 Nov 2026 21:03:00 -0300 — Petrobras aprova pagamento de R$ 15,6 bilhões a acionistas
  O Conselho de Administração da Petrobras, em reunião realizada nesta segunda-feira (23), aprovou o pagamento de remuneração extraordinária aos acionistas no valor de R$ 15, 6 bilhões, equivalente a R$ 1,21035949 por ação ordinária e preferencial em circulação. O montante será pago em parcela única e integralmente sob a forma de dividendos intercalares (durante o próprio exercício em curso, antes do fechamento do balanço anual da empresa). Para os detentores de ações de emissão da Petrobras negociadas na B3, a data-base será 7 de dezembro de 2026. As ações da Petrobras serão negociadas ex-direitos a partir do dia seguinte, 8 de dezembro de 2026. Notícias relacionadas: Petrobras tem lucro de R$ 39,8 bilhões no 3º trimestre, alta de 21,5%. O valor será pago em parcela única no dia 23 de dezem

  https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2026/11/23/2026-23838/establishment-of-class-e-airspace-columbus-tx
- Mon, 23 Nov 2026 05:00:00 GMT — Amendment of Class E Airspace; Del Rio, TX
  This action amends the Class E airspace at Del Rio, TX. This action is the result of a biennial review of the airspace associated with Laughlin Air Force Base (AFB), Del Rio, TX. This proposal also updates the name and geographic coordinates of Laughlin AFB to coincide with the FAA&#39;s aeronautical database. This action brings the airspace into compliance with FAA orders and supports instrument flight rule (IFR) procedures and operations.
  https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2026/11/23/2026-23836/amendment-of-class-e-airspace-del-rio-tx
- Mon, 23 Nov 2026 05:00:00 GMT — Amendment of Class D and E Airspace and Removal of Class E Airspace Over Lebanon, NH
  This action proposes to amend Class D and Class E airspace over Lebanon, NH. This action would increase the lateral dimensions of the Lebanon, NH, Class D airspace to appropriately contain Instrument Flight Rules (IFR) operations at the Lebanon Municipal Airport. This action would also update verbiage in the Class D airspace legal description to comply with current FAA guidance. This action would also update the geographic coordinates for the airport in both the Class D and Class E5 airspace legal descriptions. This action would also remove the exclusions for adjacent Class E5 airspace from the Lebanon, NH, Class E5 airspace legal description. This action would also remove the Lebanon, NH, Class E4 airspace, which is no longer necessary for IFR operations at the airport.
  https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2026/11/23/2026-23824/amendment-of-class-d-and-e-airspace-and-removal-of-class-e-airspace-over-lebanon-nh

#### Payload (space_press, en, newsroom)

- undated — Exclusive: Verde Technologies Will Test Perovskite Solar Cells on the ISS in 2027
  Exclusive: Verde Technologies Will Test Perovskite Solar Cells on the ISS in 2027 Skip to content Solar cell manufacturer Verde Technologies signed a contract to send its lightweight, ultra-thin perovskite solar cells to the ISS NET June, company officials told Payload. The mission—part of NASA’s Materials International Space Station Experiment (MISSE) , which focuses on testing new materials and components on the orbital lab—will involve bolting Verde’s solar cells to the outside of the ISS for six months before they’re returned to Earth for analysis. The research aims to validate Verde’s perovskite solar cell technology in space for the first time and open the door for Verde to begin producing and selling solar cells to spacecraft operators at scale. “The plan for us is to validate the technology completely, then start entering the market in steps,” CEO Jean-Noel Poirier told Payload.
  https://payloadspace.com/exclusive-verde-technologies-will-test-perovskite-solar-cells-on-the-iss-in-2027/
- undated — Industry Shares Plan for Moon Base Power Tower
  Industry Shares Plan for Moon Base Power Tower Skip to content When your phone dies on Earth, you find the nearest outlet. On the Moon, someone has to build the outlet first. NASA took a first step towards this goal at its Nov. 7 Moon Base update during MAX POWER, tapping three companies—Redwire, Blue Origin’s Honeybee Robotics, and Yank Technologies—to build the pieces of a Mini-Communications and Solar Tower (miniCAST). Redwire will generate power with its roll-out solar arrays (ROSA). Honeybee Robotics will store it. Yank Technologies will supply wireless power transfer hardware. Today, Yank Technologies shared more about its role in the project, including that it will deliver flight hardware to power payloads such as rovers. Marshall Space Flight Center will assemble and test the pieces before they head to the lunar surface. NASA expects a separate solicitation for fully integrated p
  https://payloadspace.com/industry-shares-plan-for-moon-base-power-tower/
- undated — Atomic-6 Wants to Shield Astronauts Against Untrackable Debris
  Atomic-6 Wants to Shield Astronauts Against Untrackable Debris Skip to content What’s the scariest thing about space exploration? Probably the snakes . The second most frightening thing? Untrackable debris. Atomic-6 built Space Armor to shield satellites from debris too small to track. The startup released test results today exploring whether the same tiles could protect astronauts on spacewalks. “Building Space Armor, we want to protect assets in space,” Atomic-6 CEO Trevor Smith told Payload. “I can’t think of a higher value asset than a human life.” Bullseye: At Texas A&amp;M University’s Hypervelocity Impact Laboratory, Atomic-6 fired a 3-mm aluminum sphere speeding ~7.2 km/s (~16,100 mph) at a ballistic torso—once behind a Space Armor Lite tile and once behind a 4-mm-thick solid aluminum plate.   https://payloadspace.com/atomic-6-wants-to-shield-astronauts-against-untrackable-debris/

- undated — Voyager 1 Reaches New Milestone: One Light-Day from Earth
  Voyager 1 Reaches New Milestone: One Light-Day from Earth &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com NASA&#8217;s Voyager 1 spacecraft achieved another milestone this morning. Launched in 1977, it became the first human object to pass into interstellar space in August 2012 and is so far from Earth now that it takes one entire day for a radio signal to get there &#8212; one light-day. It also takes one light-day for Voyager 1 to send back the data it is still acquiring after all these decades. Voyager 1 passed the one light-day threshold at 5:16:07 am ET this morning at a distance of 16.094 billion miles (25.902 billion kilometers). That means it takes two days to communicate with Voyager &#8212; one day for the signal to get there and another day for the answer to come back. The spacecraft sent back stunning images of Jupiter in 1979 and Saturn in 1980. Since then it&#8217;s been on a quest to find w
  https://spacepolicyonline.com/news/voyager-1-reaches-new-milestone-one-light-day-from-earth/

Committee rules state that a quorum is needed for official actions and that requires at least one Senator from the minority party, but they were approved nonetheless. President Trump nominated Joshua Shoemaker to be NASA&#8217;s Chief Financial Officer and Robert Steinau to be Inspector General, as well as Danielle Thumann Severs to be an FCC commissioner. #### Canadian Space Agency news (government, en, newsroom)

- undated — November 18, 2026 | From Whitehorse to Space: Students connect with Canadian Space Agency astronauts
  From Whitehorse to space: Students hear from Canadian Space Agency astronauts | Canadian Space Agency --> Top of page From Whitehorse to space: Students hear from Canadian Space Agency astronauts 2026-11-18 For students in Whitehorse, Yukon, space felt a little closer to home during a special event where they had the chance to hear from both Canadian Space Agency (CSA) astronauts Jenni Gibbons and Colonel Joshua Kutryk. Hosted by Jenni, the event brought together 300 students from St. Francis of Assisi Catholic Secondary School, F.H. Collins Secondary School, Porter Creek Secondary School, CSSC Mercier and Christ the King Elementary School. - undated — Defensie bouwt aan Space Command Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting ... 30-06-2026 17:00 Lees verder
  Defensie bouwt aan Space Command | Rijksoverheid.nl Nieuwsbericht 30-06-2026 | 17:00 Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting van het Space Command bouwt Defensie verder aan het vijfde operationele domein (ruimte) naast land, zee, lucht en cyber. Dit is aangekondigd in de gisteren gepresenteerde Defensienota. Het doel is om in 2028 volledig operationeel te zijn met het Space Command . De basis voor militaire ruimteactiviteiten werd ruim 10 jaar geleden gelegd met kleinschalige experimenten. Inmiddels is het ruimtedomein uitgegroeid tot een operationeel onderdeel van Defensie, onder meer dankzij de inzet van eigen observatiesatellieten. Ruimte werd vorig jaar officieel ingericht als vijfde operationele domein. Daarom volgde de transitie van het Commando Luchtstrijdkrachten (CLSK) naar het Commando Lucht
  https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2026/06/30/defensie-bouwt-aan-space-command

- Thu, 19 Nov 2026 11:10:00 +0100 — Watch live: Hera’s first images of post-impact asteroid Dimorphos
  A little more than four years ago, humans moved an asteroid, as NASA’s DART spacecraft impacted a body called Dimorphos. Now the European Space Agency’s Hera spacecraft has arrived at that same asteroid to find out what happened next. Join the Hera team on the evening of Wednesday 25 November as they share the first close-up images of this historic transformed space object.

Su misi&oacute;n principal es servir de nexo entre la tecnolog&iacute;a espacial y los usuarios, garantizando la explotaci&oacute;n de sistemas cr&iacute;ticos como Galileo y EGNOS, y fomentando el desarrollo de mercados y aplicaciones derivadas. EUSPA se erige como un pilar estrat&eacute;gico para la autonom&iacute;a y el liderazgo espacial europeo . Entre sus competencias clave se encuentran asegurar la acreditaci&oacute;n de seguridad para todas las acciones

La AEE tiene el mandato de unificar la postura nacional, aportando coherencia, coordinaci&oacute;n y una visi&oacute;n estrat&eacute;gica integral a la participaci&oacute;n de Espa&ntilde;a en el exterior. Esta labor asegura que los intereses cient&iacute;ficos, industriales y gubernamentales espa&ntilde;oles se defiendan de manera un&iacute;voca, tanto en los grandes foros multilaterales como en la relaci&oacute;n directa con otras potencias espaciales.   BepiColombo, inserimento in orbita con imprevisto &#8211; MEDIA INAF Dopo otto anni e 10,2 miliardi di chilometri percorsi, sabato 21 novembre 2026 la missione dell’Agenzia spaziale europea (Esa) BepiColombo è entrata in orbita attorno a Mercurio. A dare la notizia, un post su X pubblicato dalla stessa agenzia che confermava il completamento della manovra e la ricezione del segnale da parte della sonda. In un post successivo pubblicato il giorno seguente, però, la stessa agenzia ha chiarito che non tutto sarebbe andato come previsto: un&#8217;anomalia software avrebbe provocato l&#8217;interruzione anticipata dell&#8217;accensione dei propulsori, costringendo la sonda a entrare in modalità di sicurezza.   Chi mangia patate (lunari) non muore mai &#8211; MEDIA INAF Una delle principali sfide dell’esplorazione spaziale dei prossimi decenni è stabilire una presenza umana stabile e duratura sul nostro satellite naturale, un obiettivo a cui punta il programma Artemis della Nasa, che mira anche a gettare le basi per la futura esplorazione di Marte. Affinché ciò sia possibile, sarà tuttavia necessario garantire agli astronauti un approvvigionamento alimentare sostenibile. La permanenza prolungata sulla Luna dipenderà infatti anche dalla possibilità di produrre cibo direttamente sul posto, riducendo la necessità di costose missioni di rifornimento dalla Terra. Patate ottenute negli esperimenti condotti da David Handy e colleghi in un terreno simulante la regolite lunare.   Face à Starlink, Orange, Deutsche Telekom, Telefonica et Vodafone veulent créer un service mobile par satellite européen et souverain. Mais les délais, le coût et le manque de solutions disponibles compliquent leur projet.

- undated — Was genau ist ein CubeSat? : Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders?
  Was genau ist ein CubeSat? - Austria in Space Was genau ist ein CubeSat? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders? Früher waren Satelliten immer riesengroße, tonnenschwere Raumfahrzeuge, die ein hohes Budget, viel Zeit und viel Expertise verlangt haben. Nur wenige Nationen konntent sich solche Raumfahrt-Technologie leisten, was zur Folge hatte, dass es nur eine handvoll Weltraumnationen gab. Heute ist das anders, und das liegt an einem Würfel mit 10 cm Seitenlänge! Richtig, die Rede ist von einem CubeSat. Ein CubeSat, das ist ein kleiner Satellit, der aus einem oder mehreren würfelförmigen Modulen mit eben jeweils nur 10 cm Seitenlänge. Dabei sind diese Raumfahrzeuge nicht nur klein, sondern auch sehr leicht: Eine Einheit wiegt maximal 1,33 Kilo.
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/was-sind-cubesats.php
- undated — Was versteht man unter Spacetech? : Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau?
  Was versteht man unter Spacetech? - Austria in Space Was versteht man unter Spacetech? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau? Jeder redet von Space Technology, aber was ist das überhaupt?&nbsp; Der Weltraumsektor ist im Wesentlichen zweigeteilt: In Upstream und in Downstream. Der Upstream Bereich umfasst die Beförderung von Objekten in den Weltraum, Bodenstationen und die Weltraumforschung, während als Downstream Daten und Services bezeichnet werden, die sich aus dem Upstream, vor allem aus Satelliten, ergeben.&nbsp;Eine Vielzahl an Anwendungen für die Erde fallen daher in den Downstream Bereich. Gerade darin liegt auch die Stärke des österreichischen Weltraumsektors. Doch auch europaweit liegt dort das größte Potential für die Wirtsch
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/artikel/was-ist-spacetech.php

#### BMIMI (Federal Ministry for Innovation, Mobility and Infrastructure) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen
  Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Das Weltraumbüro (UNOOSA) und der Weltraumausschuss (UNCOPUOS) wurden 1958 von der Generalversammlung der Vereinten Nationen gegründet. Ziel des Ausschusses ist es, die friedliche internationale Zusammenarbeit im Weltraum zu fördern, die Erforschung des Weltraums zu unterstützen, sowie das Weltraumrecht weiterzuentwickeln. Österreich ist seit 1959 Mitglied des Weltraumausschusses. Seit 1993 befinden sich das Büro und der Ausschuss in Wien. Jährlich treffen sich Delegierte aus mittlerweile 100 Mitgliedsstaaten in Wien, um aktuelle und zukünftige Themen und Entwicklungen der Weltraumforschung und -nutzung zu diskutieren.&#xa0; Das Bundesministerium unterstützt die Arbeiten des Weltraumbüros insbesondere mit dem jährlich stattfindenden United Nations/Austria Symposium in Graz, sowie dem World Space Forum.
  https://www.bmimi.gv.at/themen/innovation/fti-themen/weltraum-und-luftfahrttechnologien/weltraum/weltraumbuero.html

- undated — pdf
  24 déc 2025 -16:21 > Appartient à Conseil des ministres du 23 décembre 2025 ## Programmation spatiale belge pour la période 2026-2030 Sur proposition de la ministre chargée de la Politique scientifique Vanessa Matz, le Conseil des ministres a pris acte des récents développements dans le secteur spatial international et des opportunités qui s'offrent aux entreprises spatiales belges dans ce même contexte. Il prend par ailleurs plusieurs décisions destinées à renforcer le secteur spatial belge. Les 26 et 27 novembre s’est tenue à Brême la réunion du conseil de l’Agence spatiale européenne (ESA) au niveau ministériel, qui couvrait tous les domaines d’activités pour la période 2026-2030. Au cours de cette réunion, les États membres étaient invités à adopter quatre résolutions et à s’inscrire aux différents programmes pour les cinq prochaines années. Dans ce contexte, le Conseil des ministres : approuve le cofinancement par la Défense de la participation belge aux programmes de l’ESA a dégagé 400 millions d’euros lors de l’élaboration du budget pluriannuel afin de permettre aux inscriptions belges d'atteindre un niveau satisfaisant et de d’octroyer à l'écosystème spatial belge suffisamm

  Nederland verhoogt inzet tijdens ESA-ministersconferentie in Bremen - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Minister van Economische Zaken, Vincent Karremans, op ESA-ministerconferentie Nederland verhoogt de bijdrage aan de Europese ruimtevaartorganisatie ESA met 109 miljoen euro. Dit kondigde minister Karremans van Economische Zaken vandaag aan tijdens de ESA ministersconferentie in Bremen. De totale Nederlandse inzet tijdens de conferentie komt daarmee op 453 miljoen euro. NSO-directeur Harm van de Wetering reageert: “Hiermee zet Nederland een belangrijke stap in lijn met de politiek gesteunde Langetermijn-ruimtevaartagenda.” Eens in de drie jaar komen de voor ruimtevaart verantwoordelijke ministers van ESA-lidstaten bij elkaar tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie. Daar besluiten ze over de toekomst van de Europese ruimtevaart. Dit jaar vond de conferentie plaats in Bremen. Op
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nederland-verhoogt-inzet-tijdens-esa-ministersconferentie-in-bremen/

  Wat als…? Betere besluitvorming mogelijk met Digitale tweeling DestinE - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Zeven jaar en vijfhonderd miljoen euro. Dat investeert de Europese Commissie in de ontwikkeling van de ‘digitale tweeling’ Destination Earth. Maar wat kun je met zo’n tweeling? En hoe onderscheidt hij zich van andere informatiediensten? Daarover ging de eerste nationale DestinE Living Lab dag in Amsterdam. &nbsp; Destination Earth ( DestinE ) is een digitale representatie van onze planeet. Een simulatie die de wisselwerking tussen natuur, klimaat en menselijk handelen op onze planeet zo nauwkeurig mogelijk nabootst. Dat doet hij op basis van verschillende databronnen – waaronder satellietdata van het Europese aardobservatieprogramma Copernicus – en met behulp van geavanceerde klimaat- en weermodellen, supercomputers en AI.&nbsp; ‘Satellietgegevens helpen ons te begrijpe

  Aan de Voorzitter van de Tweede Kamer der Staten-Generaal Den Haag, 27 januari 2026 Met deze brief informeer ik uw Kamer over de uitkomsten van de Ministeriële Conferentie 2025 (MC25) van de European Space Agency (ESA), die op 26 en 27 november 2025 in Bremen (Duitsland) heeft plaatsgevonden. Ik stuur deze uitkomsten mede namens de Minister van Onderwijs, Cultuur en Wetenschap en de Minister van Defensie. Daarnaast stuur ik uw Kamer een kabinetsreactie op de uitgevoerde beleidsevaluatie van het civiele ruimtevaartbeleid van 2017 tot en met 2024. De Minister van Economische Zaken,V.P.G.Karremans **Uitkomst MC25** De uitkomst van MC25 laat zien dat Europese landen ruimtevaart nadrukkelijk zien als een strategische randvoorwaarde. In de huidige geopolitieke context speelt de ruimte een steeds grotere rol voor onze veiligheid en weerbaarheid, terwijl satellietinfrastructuur en satellietdata ook voor onze samenleving vitaal zijn: voor logistiek, landbouw, (veilige) communicatie, weersvoorspellingen, klimaatonderzoek en dijkbewaking. - undated — Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space
  Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space Menu It has become a truism that Europe’s defence industry and military systems are excessively fragmented, and therefore often more costly and less efficient than they would be if pooled at the European, or at least regional, level. Yet despite repeated warnings and countless recommendations, the continent now appears set to repeat the same mistake in space security, leaving itself particularly exposed at precisely the moment when space is becoming more strategically important than ever. European space capabilities are already deeply fragmented, especially in the defence sector. In contrast to continent-wide civilian programmes such as Galileo or Copernicus, military space assets remain scattered across the armed forces and governments of individual states. As a result, just four countries — France, Germany, Italy, and Spain — operate

For analysts and planners, however, it remains a maritime area riddled with gaps — in legislation, procedures and the architecture for sharing information. The recent ORKAN 26 exercise sought to identify those gaps before an adversary could exploit them. #### Space24 (space_press, pl, newsroom)

- undated — Przechwycony Starship nie poleci już w kosmos
  Przechwycony Starship nie poleci już w kosmos Menu Ship 42 uczestniczył w piętnastym locie testowym systemu Starship/Super Heavy. Około 22 godzin po starcie górny stopień powrócił do Starbase, gdzie został przechwycony przez mechaniczne ramiona wieży startowej Mechazilla. Było to pierwsze w historii lotów kosmicznych udane przechwycenie górnego stopnia rakiety nośnej bezpośrednio przez wieżę startową . SpaceX odzyskało drugi stopień Starshipa Warto zauważyć, że Starship był w stanie wyhamować z prędkości ponad 26 tys. km/h , przetrwać wejście w atmosferę i zwolnić do zaledwie kilkuset km/h na wysokości około kilometra nad Ziemią. Właśnie wtedy wykonał manewr obrotu do pozycji pionowej, tzw. „flipa”, uruchamiając trzy silniki Raptor, co pozwoliło na jego kompletne wyhamowanie i udane przechwycenie. Mechazilla catches Starship! Congratulations to the entire SpaceX team on this incredible a
  https://space24.pl/pojazdy-kosmiczne/systemy-nosne/przechwycony-starship-nie-poleci-juz-w-kosmos
- undated — Rosja rozmieszcza pociski Sarmat. Są zdjęcia satelitarne
  Rosja rozmieszcza pociski Sarmat. Są zdjęcia satelitarne Menu Działania rosyjskich sił strategicznych potwierdzają zdjęcia satelitarne , opublikowane w analizie brytyjskiego ośrodka International Institute for Strategic Studies (IISS), na który powołuje się Militarnyj w opublikowanym artykule. Przedstawiają one przygotowania do załadunku pocisków RS-28 do silosów startowych w rosyjskiej bazie wojskowej w Kraju Krasnojarskim na Syberii. Baza znajduje się w pobliżu miasta Użur ; należy do 62. Dywizji Strategicznych Wojsk Rakietowych. Satellite imagery suggests that Russia’s nuclear-armed RS-28 Sarmat (RS-SS-X-29) ICBM may have begun to enter service with the Strategic Rocket Forces. Further Sarmat flight trials may also take place as Moscow looks to ease its reliability concerns. Read the latest #MissileDialogueInitiative analysis by Matthew Bint: https://t.co/3RLFaxdCNy &mdash; IISS News
  https://space24.pl/bezpieczenstwo/technologie-wojskowe/rosja-rozmieszcza-pociski-sarmat-sa-zdjecia-satelitarne
- undated — Sonda BepiColombo weszła na orbitę wokół Merkurego
  Sonda BepiColombo weszła na orbitę wokół Merkurego Menu 21 listopada br., zbliżając się do południowego bieguna najmniejszej planety Układu Słonecznego , sonda BepiColombo uruchomiła swoje cztery silniki w celu zmniejszenia prędkości. W wyniku manewru statek kosmiczny został przechwycony przez grawitację Merkurego i wszedł na jego orbitę jako dopiero drugi w historii, po amerykańskiej sondzie MESSENGER, która dokonała tego w 2011 r. Powtórzenie tego osiągnięcia wiązało się z przebyciem ponad 10 miliardów kilometrów i wykonaniem dziewięciu asyst grawitacyjnych, które pozwoliły sondzie wytracić wystarczająco dużo prędkości, aby zbliżyć ją do tej, z jaką porusza się Merkury. Nie obyło się po drodze bez przeszkód, ponieważ awaria systemu zasilania modułu transportowego w kwietniu 2024 r. ograniczyła moc jego silników jonowych, co ostatecznie wydłużyło całą kosmiczną tułaczkę o kolejny rok.   https://space24.pl/pojazdy-kosmiczne/sondy/bepicolombo-wszedl-na-orbite-wokol-merkurego

Velik korak za civilizacijo, je podvig komentiral generalni direktor Evropske vesoljske postaje. Gre za prvi pristanek na kometu v zgodovini človeštva in zgodovinski dosežek Evropske vesoljske agencije (ESA). Do pristanka je prišlo nekaj minut po peti popoldne po našem času. Po ločitvi Philae in Rosette so znanstveniki v Nemčiji prejeli signal z Rosette, ki jim je začela pošiljati podatke, in tudi s Philae. Philae so tako lahko sledili vso pot do površja kometa. Zgodovinski poskus pristanka na kometu s premerom več kot štiri kilometre se je odvijal več kot 500 milijonov kilometrov stran od Zemlje.   Senators Introduce Legislation to Streamline Satellite Licensing - Via Satellite : Home » Government/Military » Senators Introduce Legislation to Streamline Satellite Licensing --> U.S. Capitol Building. Photo: Wikimedia. Senators introduced bipartisan legislation this week aimed at streamlining the application process for new satellites. Senators Ted Cruz, R-Texas, and Peter Welch, D-Vermont, introduced the Satellite and Telecommunications Streamlining Act on January 14 which would set a one-year deadline for the FCC to make a decision on a license application. The stated goal of the legislation is to incentivize commercial satellite operators to base operations in the United States over foreign jurisdictions where satellite application processes may be less burdensome.   https://www.satellitetoday.com/government-military/2026/01/16/senators-introduce-legislation-to-streamline-satellite-licensing/
- undated — Telesat Partners With Canada’s Dominion Dynamics for Portable and Uncrewed LEO Antennas
  Telesat Partners With Canada’s Dominion Dynamics for Portable and Uncrewed LEO Antennas - Via Satellite : Home » Government/Military » Telesat Partners With Canada’s Dominion Dynamics for Portable and Uncrewed LEO Antennas --> Rendering of a Dominion Dynamics Scout ACP. Photo: Dominion Dynamics Telesat is working with Canadian defense tech company Dominion Dynamics on ruggedized antennas for military use for the Telesat Lightspeed constellation, including a terminal for uncrewed systems. Dominion Dynamics is a Canadian defense-tech startup based in Ottawa, founded in 2025.   ### 1. Citation This Order may be cited as the Kenya Space Agency Order. ### 2. Interpretation In this Order—"Act" means the State Corporations Act (Cap. 466);"Agency" means the Kenya Space Agency established under paragraph 3;"Board" means the Board of the Agency appointed under paragraph 6;"Director-General" means the person appointed under paragraph 11;"Cabinet Secretary" means the Cabinet Secretary for the time being responsible for Defence;"Committee" means the State Corporations Advisory Committee;"member" means a person appointed to the Board under paragraph 6(1)(i). ## Part II – ESTABLISHMENT, POWERS AND FUNCTIONS OF KENYA SPACE AGENCY ### 3.   https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/ln/2017/22

- undated — MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate
  MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate Sep 06, 2026 Destaques O Space Enabled Research Group, integrado no MIT Media Lab do Massachusetts Institute of Technology (MIT), estendeu a afiliação do angolano Dr. Zolana João como Research Affiliate (Investigador Afiliado), vínculo que mantém com o grupo desde 2023. A decisão dá continuidade a uma colaboração científica desenvolvida ao longo dos últimos anos com uma das instituições académicas de maior referência mundial nas áreas de ciência, tecnologia e engenharia. A extensão da afiliação reforça a presença de Angola em redes internacionais de investigação e inovação e reconhece a contribuição de investigadores angolanos para a produção de conhecimento sobre a utilização responsável, inclusiva e sustentável das tecno
  https://ggpen.gov.ao/en/news/mit-space-enabled-estende-afiliacao-do-angolano-zolana-joao-como-research-affiliate
- undated — Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space
  Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Jul 23, 2026 Destaques Foi lançado, no dia 13 de Julho de 2026, o projecto Conecta Angola TVWS, uma iniciativa do MINTTICS, através do GGPEN e parceiros, que combina as capacidades do satélite ANGOSAT-2 com a tecnologia TV White Space (TVWS) para levar internet de banda larga a comunidades com acesso limitado à conectividade. A solução utiliza frequências não ocupadas do espectro televisivo para fornecer internet de forma eficiente, segura e com alcance de até 10 quilómetros, permitindo ampliar a cobertura digital e criar novas oportunidades de acesso a plataformas educativas, conteúdos digitais e serviços tecnológicos. - undated — Training – A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System
  NEREUS Training &#8211; A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System &#8211; Mauritius Research and Innovation Council Skip to content Menu This training is the third and last of a series of courses within the Capacity Building programme for the NEREUS project funded by the United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) Ocean Innovation Challenge ( https://oceaninnovationchallenge.org ). The NEREUS project, led by the Surrey Space Centre (SSC) in collaboration with the Mauritius Research and Innovation Council (MRIC), aims at developing a tool, based on a Machine Learning algorithm to detect anomalies in maritime satellite data. Analysing these anomalies is very important in ensuring maritime security and environmental protection. Overview Who we are The Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency (ZINGSA) is a wholly government-owned entity established in terms of the Research Act [Chapter 10:22] and officially launched by President Emmerson Mnangagwa in 2021. ZINGSA is responsible for promoting the peaceful use of space and applying geospatial and space technologies to support Zimbabwe&#8217;s socio-economic development and industrialization. ZIMSAT-1 Zimbabwe&#8217;s first satellite, successfully launched in November 2022. Used for monitoring drought conditions, mapping mining activities, assessing crop health, and generating accurate weather forecasts. ZIMSAT-2 Zimbabwe&#8217;s second satellite mission, building on the success of ZIMSAT-1 and expanding national capabilities in space technology and Earth observation. CubeSat Development Advanced CubeSat development using 3D printing technology and modern engineering practices to build indigenous small-satellite capability. - undated — 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について New!
  国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について : 宇宙政策 - 内閣府 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について 令和８年９月１日 内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局 １．概要 令和８年８月31日（月）、龍崎孝嗣内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局長は、「内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局と米国連邦航空局（FAA）※との間の国際商業宇宙輸送に係る協力覚書」に署名を行いました。本協力覚書には、日本側から龍崎局長が署名を行い、米国側からベッドフォード連邦航空局長官が署名を行いました。 覚書締結に合わせて、龍崎局長から、本協力覚書を通じて、日米両国の法令や基準の調和を図り、日米共に更に安全で信頼性の高い商業宇宙輸送の拡大を目指す旨発言しました。ベッドフォード長官からも、引き続き連携を深めていく意向が示され、今後の協力の継続及び定期的な会合の実施を両者で確認しました。 ※米国連邦航空局（Federal Aviation Administration：FAA）は、米国における商業宇宙輸送活動の奨励、円滑化及び促進を図るため、商業宇宙輸送を規制する権限を有している。 ２．協力覚書の目的 本協力覚書は、商業宇宙輸送に適用されるベストプラクティスの共有及び産業標準の推進を行い、相互の関心事項及びプロジェクトに関して協力することを目的としています。 ３．対象とする協力活動概要 本協力覚書が推進する協力は、主に以下の項目です。 法令、規制、政策、プログラム及びプロジェクトに関する情報交換等 宇宙機の打上げ及び再突入、サブオービタル飛行、有人宇宙飛行並びに宇宙港に関する安全基準、リスク管理基準及び適合性評価における共通事項の検討 ベストプラクティスの共有 シンポジウム、ワークショップ、会議又はワーキンググループの共同開催 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について このページの先頭へ
  https://www8.cao.go.jp/space/kokusai/20260901/260901.html

- undated — GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit.
  GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION Home / GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit. October 28, 2026 GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 is the 20 th flight of India’s Geosynchronous Satellite Launch Vehicle (GSLV). The GSLV-F18 vehicle placed NVS-03 satellite into a Geosynchronous Transfer Orbit.The Launch was successfully executed from the Second Launch Pad (SLP) at Satish Dhawan Space Centre, SHAR. NVS-03 is the Third Satellite in Next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries Indigenous Atomic clock, along with the procured Atomic clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to existing L5/S signals with Coverage over India land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. GSLV-F18 on the launch pad More De
  https://www.isro.gov.in/Mission_GSLVF18.html

Home / Hosting of 20 th International Committee on GNSS (ICG-20), Goa, India. November 13, 2026 International Committee on Global Navigation Satellite Systems (ICG), established in 2005 under the umbrella of the United Nations is an important forum to promote voluntary cooperation on matters of mutual interest related to civil satellite-based positioning, navigation, timing, and value-added services. ISRO/DoS had the privilege to host the 20 th annual meeting of ICG (ICG-20) from October 25 to 30, 2026 along with the United Nations Office for Outer Space Affairs (UNOOSA) at Goa, India. Subsequent to the anomalies observed in the third stage of PSLV during the PSLV-C61/EOS-09 & PSLV-C62 / EOS-N1 missions, the configuration of the PS3 stage was revisited comprehensively w.r.t the design margins, raw material acceptance criteria, assembly procedures, and the quality checks that were followed so far.   โอกาสอนาคตเศรษฐกิจอวกาศ | ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล Login หน้าแรก Tech Innovation โอกาสอนาคตเศรษฐกิจอวกาศ | ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล By ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล | สถาบันอนาคตไทยศึกษา By ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล | สถาบันอนาคตไทยศึกษา วิสัยทัศน์อนาคตด้านอวกาศ ครอบคลุมทั้งการปกป้องโลกให้ดีขึ้น การนำประโยชน์จากอวกาศมาใช้งานในโลก รวมทั้งการแสวงหาความเป็นไปได้อื่นๆ ที่มนุษย์ยังไปไม่ถึง ทิศทางอนาคต จากผู้นำเอกชนระดับโลกด้านอวกาศอย่าง &ldquo; อีลอน มัสก์ &rdquo; ผู้ก่อตั้ง บริษัทสเปซเอ็กซ์ (SpaceX) วางวิสัยทัศน์ไปสู่การตั้งถิ่นฐานของมนุษย์โลกที่ดาวอังคาร ในขณะที่ผู้นำภาคธุรกิจอย่าง &ldquo; เจฟฟ์ เบซอส &rdquo; จาก บริษัทบลู ออริจิน (Blue Origin) มีแผนอนาคตที่จะทำให้ดวงจันทร์เป็นอาณานิคมแห่งใหม่ของโลก เพื่อตอบโจทย์ข้อท้าทายที่โลกไม่สามารถดำเนินการได้ จากวิสัยทัศน์อนาคตของผู้นำระดับโลกเหล่านี้ เป็นกุญแจสำคัญในการต่อยอดโอกาสของ เศรษฐกิจอวกาศที่นานาประเทศจับตามอง 5 แนวทาง ได้แก่ 1. การเดินทางสู่อวกาศ (Going to space)

Thailand is tapping Nasa expertise to set up its northern “space lab” in Chiang Rai province. Thailand’s space agency will support the Royal Thai Armed Forces under a technology-sharing agreement announced on Monday. #### TechNews (科技新報) (press, zh, newsroom)

- undated — 閃避太空垃圾年耗 5,800 萬美元，新創 BQP 打造「衛星自動駕駛」獲美軍青睞
  閃避太空垃圾年耗 5,800 萬美元，新創 BQP 打造「衛星自動駕駛」獲美軍青睞 | TechNews 科技新報 隨著美國軌道空間日益擁擠，衛星為了閃避太空碎片所付出的代價正節節攀升。根據 NASA 估算，衛星每次為避開碎片而改變軌道，平均會消耗價值約 100 萬美元的燃料；若放眼美國整體衛星系統，每年的相關避險成本高達 5,800 萬美元。 為解決這項昂貴難題，總部位於紐約州雪城、2020 年發跡於印度的模擬技術新創 BQP，正試圖透過軟體突圍。BQP 創辦人兼執行長邱普拉（Abhishek Chopra）將這套系統稱為「衛星專用的自動駕駛」，其核心概念是賦予衛星自主判斷避險時機的能力，打破過去完全仰賴地面人員逐一監控與下達指令的限制。 邱普拉指出，BQP 的系統已在合作夥伴的硬體上順利完成地面測試，接下來將送往國際太空站，在真實的太空環境中進行進一步驗證。該公司主打的技術優勢在於驚人的運算效率：能將原本需要約 40 天才能完成的軌道物體模擬，大幅縮短至 30 分鐘以內。相較於業界廣泛使用的開源工具 Orekit，其運算速度最高可飆升 2,000 倍。 目前，美國太空軍負責追蹤軌道上約 6 萬個物體，並免費向全球衛星營運商發送碰撞警示。然而，邱普拉直言，現行作業仍高度仰賴人工，當營運商收到疑似碰撞警告時，往往陷入兩難：究竟該耗費珍貴燃料進行閃避？還是冒著碰撞風險選擇不作為？隨著越來越多衛星被送入軌道，營運商面臨的決策壓力只會日益沉重。 在技術底層，BQP 採用了「量子啟發」（quantum-inspired）方法，也就是借鑒量子運算的數學思路，但讓系統能直接在客戶現有的常規晶片上執行。憑藉這項技術，該公司於今年 7 月成功拿下美國太空軍創新單位 SpaceWERX 的小型企業研究獎助。目前第一階段計畫已經完成，BQP 正積極爭取第二階段的支持。邱普拉強調，一旦挺進第二階段，這項產品將更接近可實際部署的作戰能力。 在資金布局上，BQP 於今年 9 月獲得 IBM 創投（IBM Ventures）青睞，將總募資額推升至 800 萬美元。邱普拉表示，這筆投資有效加速了商務
  https://technews.tw/2026/11/23/satellite-autonomous-driving-saves-astronomical-costs/

- Tue, 24 Nov 2026 04:02:03 GMT — Botched alerts over Russian aircraft near Ulleung Island spark confusion
  A series of contradictory emergency alerts sent to Ulleung Island residents Tuesday morning raised questions about South Korea’s disaster messaging system after Russian military aircraft approached the island through the Korea Air Defense Identification Zone (KADIZ). According to the Joint Chiefs of Staff (JCS), several Russian military aircraft entered the KADIZ over the East Sea early Tuesday and approached Ulleung Island before leaving the zone. The Air Force deployed F-15K fighters in response and the Russian aircraft withdrew eastward. The JCS stressed that no violation of South Korea’s sovereign airspace occurred.   https://www.koreatimes.co.kr/southkorea/defense/20261124/botched-alerts-over-russian-aircraft-near-ulleung-island-spark-confusion?utm_source=rss
- Tue, 24 Nov 2026 04:02:03 GMT — After SpaceX's volatile debut, ETF managers take more cautious approach toward Anthropic
  Months after SpaceX’s volatile Nasdaq debut inflicted steep losses on several Korean exchange-traded funds (ETFs), fund managers are taking a more measured approach to Anthropic, which also saw its shares swing in their first days of trading, according to industry officials Monday. The artificial intelligence (AI) company behind the Claude chatbot set a new record for the largest initial public offering (IPO) in history when it raised $81.3 billion during its Nov. 19 Nasdaq debut, surpassing that of SpaceX. Its shares closed at $97.17 on the first day, 17.1 percent above the $83 offering price. But the stock retreated the following day, falling as low as $83.05 before closing at $88.91. The sharp swings have put the spotlight on a key decision for domestic ETF managers to gauge the fund’s
